Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any hilbert module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 762. The projective resolution has length at most 573 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on hilbert structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 762. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 573. QED.
